## Raw Results

Status of all evaluations across models. Color coding:

- 🟢 Passing 100% (stable)
- 🟡 Passing 1-99%
- 🔴 Passing 0% (failing)
- 🔧 Mock data failure (missing or invalid test data)
- ⚠️ Setup or model-provider failure (environment/infrastructure issue; the model never answered, so the run is left out of success rates)
- ⏱️ Timeout or rate limit error (left out of success rates)
- ⏭️ Test skipped (e.g., known issue or precondition not met)
